([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mahalanobis-distance-mapping-metrics))
- **mahalanobis-distance-outlier-detection**: Use when you must call statistically significant compartment differences between two or more Hi-C contact maps — including changes within the same A or B compartment type that standard PCA-on-each-map analysis cannot detect. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/mahalanobis-distance-outlier-detection))
- **mahalanobis-distance-significance-testing**: Use when comparing compartment scores of 100 kb (or finer, down to 10 kb) genomic bins across two or more replicate-grouped Hi-C contact maps (e.g., mouse ESC vs NPC) and needing statistically significant differential compartment calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/mahalanobis-distance-significance-testing))
- **mahalanobis-like-between-cluster-distance-computation**: Use when you have cell-to-cluster assignments plus a low-dimensional SingleCellExperiment embedding and need between-cluster distances that account for within-cluster covariance, to build an MST cluster topology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mahalanobis-like-between-cluster-distance-computation))
